{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01Qt6hburpVHrGvpujPY7WfC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01WsVoYmiUcAP55Wy2DbTP7q","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/solve_acopf.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC OPF) Solver\nImplements the mathematical model from math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\n\ndef load_network(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef build_acopf_model(data):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data (MATPOWER format)\n    # Columns: bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i][0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    # Columns: bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    # Columns: fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data (quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    # Create optimization variables\n    # Bus voltages: V = Vm * exp(j*Va)\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator power outputs\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    # Collect all decision variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build objective function: sum of quadratic generator costs\n    obj = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        cost_type = int(gencost_data[i][0])  # 2 = polynomial\n        n_coeffs = int(gencost_data[i][3])\n        if cost_type == 2 and n_coeffs == 3:\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n            c2 = gencost_data[i][4]\n            c1 = gencost_data[i][5]\n            c0 = gencost_data[i][6]\n            # Convert from per MW to per p.u. (baseMVA)\n            obj += c2 * (Pg[i] * baseMVA)**2 + c1 * (Pg[i] * baseMVA) + c0\n\n    # Build constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus constraint: angle = 0 for reference buses (type 3)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_type = int(bus_data[i][1])\n        if bus_type == 3:  # Reference bus\n            g.append(Va[i])\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance constraints for each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(bus_data[i][0])\n\n        # Load at this bus (convert to p.u.)\n        Pd = bus_data[i][2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus_data[i][3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Shunt admittance at this bus (convert to p.u.)\n        Gs = bus_data[i][4] / baseMVA\n        Bs = bus_data[i][5] / baseMVA\n\n        # Generation at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for j in range(n_gen):\n            gen_bus_id = int(gen_data[j][0])\n            gen_status = int(gen_data[j][7])\n            if gen_bus_id == bus_id and gen_status == 1:\n                Pg_bus += Pg[j]\n                Qg_bus += Qg[j]\n\n        # Shunt power injection\n        S_shunt_real = Gs * Vm[i]**2\n        S_shunt_imag = -Bs * Vm[i]**2  # Capacitive shunt is positive Q\n\n        # Branch flows connected to this bus\n        P_flow = 0\n        Q_flow = 0\n\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n            tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n            f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            # Branch parameters (in p.u.)\n            r = branch_data[k][2]\n            x = branch_data[k][3]\n            b = branch_data[k][4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = branch_data[k][8]\n            shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0  # Convert to radians\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n            g_series = np.real(y_series)\n            b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n            # Shunt charging (split equally on both ends)\n            b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n            # Complex tap ratio\n            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n            tap_mag = abs(tap_complex)\n            tap_angle = np.angle(tap_complex)\n\n            # From-end power flow (i -> j)\n            if fbus_id == bus_id:\n                # Voltage angle difference\n                theta_ij = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx] - tap_angle\n\n                # From-end power flow\n                P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                       Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n\n                Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                       Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n\n                P_flow += P_ij\n                Q_flow += Q_ij\n\n            # To-end power flow (j -> i)\n            if tbus_id == bus_id:\n                # Voltage angle difference\n                theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + tap_angle\n\n                # To-end power flow\n                P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                       Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n\n                Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 + \\\n                       Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n\n                P_flow += P_ji\n                Q_flow += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Flow out\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - S_shunt_real - P_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - S_shunt_imag - Q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        status = int(gen_data[i][7])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        Pmin = gen_data[i][9] / baseMVA\n        Pmax = gen_data[i][8] / baseMVA\n        Qmin = gen_data[i][4] / baseMVA\n        Qmax = gen_data[i][3] / baseMVA\n\n        h.append(Pg[i])\n        lbh.append(Pmin)\n        ubh.append(Pmax)\n\n        h.append(Qg[i])\n        lbh.append(Qmin)\n        ubh.append(Qmax)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vmin = bus_data[i][12]\n        Vmax = bus_data[i][11]\n\n        h.append(Vm[i])\n        lbh.append(Vmin)\n        ubh.append(Vmax)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n        f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n        t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data[k][2]\n        x = branch_data[k][3]\n        b = branch_data[k][4]\n\n        # Flow limit (MVA rating)\n        rateA = branch_data[k][5]\n        if rateA > 0:\n            rateA_pu = rateA / baseMVA\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = branch_data[k][8]\n            shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n            g_series = np.real(y_series)\n            b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n            # Shunt charging\n            b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n            # Complex tap ratio\n            tap_mag = tap\n            tap_angle = shift\n\n            # From-end flow\n            theta_ij = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx] - tap_angle\n\n            P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n\n            Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n            h.append(S_ij)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rateA_pu)\n\n            # To-end flow\n            theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + tap_angle\n\n            P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n\n            Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 + \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n\n            S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(S_ji)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rateA_pu)\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        angmin = branch_data[k][11] * np.pi / 180.0\n        angmax = branch_data[k][12] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n        if angmin > -360 or angmax < 360:\n            fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n            tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n            f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            h.append(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n            lbh.append(angmin)\n            ubh.append(angmax)\n\n    # Assemble constraints\n    g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n    constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n\n    lbg_vec = np.array(lbg + lbh)\n    ubg_vec = np.array(ubg + ubh)\n\n    # Create NLP problem\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n\n    # Voltage magnitudes: initialize to 1.0 p.u.\n    x0[:n_bus] = 1.0\n\n    # Voltage angles: initialize to 0\n    x0[n_bus:2*n_bus] = 0\n\n    # Generator real power: distribute total load + estimated losses\n    total_load = sum(bus_data[i][2] for i in range(n_bus)) / baseMVA\n    avg_pg = total_load * 1.05 / n_gen  # Add 5% for losses\n    x0[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] = avg_pg\n\n    # Generator reactive power: initialize to 0\n    x0[2*n_bus+n_gen:] = 0\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = np.ones(x.shape[0]) * -np.inf\n    ubx = np.ones(x.shape[0]) * np.inf\n\n    # No explicit bounds on Vm and Va (handled by constraints)\n    # But set reasonable bounds to help solver\n    lbx[:n_bus] = 0.5  # Vm min\n    ubx[:n_bus] = 1.5  # Vm max\n    lbx[n_bus:2*n_bus] = -np.pi  # Va min\n    ubx[n_bus:2*n_bus] = np.pi   # Va max\n\n    return {\n        'nlp': nlp,\n        'x0': x0,\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg_vec,\n        'ubg': ubg_vec,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(model_dict):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem using IPOPT\"\"\"\n\n    # IPOPT solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'print_time': True\n    }\n\n    # Create solver\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', model_dict['nlp'], opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(\n        x0=model_dict['x0'],\n        lbx=model_dict['lbx'],\n        ubx=model_dict['ubx'],\n        lbg=model_dict['lbg'],\n        ubg=model_dict['ubg']\n    )\n\n    return sol, solver\n\ndef extract_results(sol, model_dict):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format optimization results\"\"\"\n\n    x_sol = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = model_dict['n_bus']\n    n_gen = model_dict['n_gen']\n    n_branch = model_dict['n_branch']\n    baseMVA = model_dict['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = model_dict['bus_data']\n    gen_data = model_dict['gen_data']\n    branch_data = model_dict['branch_data']\n    bus_id_to_idx = model_dict['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Vm_sol = x_sol[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_sol[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_sol[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    # Convert to degrees\n    Va_deg = Va_sol * 180.0 / np.pi\n\n    # Compute total generation and load\n    total_pg = sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_qg = sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA\n\n    total_pd = sum(bus_data[i][2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_qd = sum(bus_data[i][3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    gencost_data = np.array(model_dict.get('gencost_data', []))\n    if len(gencost_data) == 0:\n        # Need to reload from original data\n        total_cost = float(sol['f'])\n    else:\n        total_cost = float(sol['f'])\n\n    # Generator results\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus_id = int(gen_data[i][0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': gen_bus_id,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_sol[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_sol[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data[i][9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data[i][8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data[i][4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data[i][3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus results\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(bus_data[i][0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm_sol[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(Va_deg[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus_data[i][12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus_data[i][11])\n        })\n\n    # Branch loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n        f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n        t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data[k][2]\n        x = branch_data[k][3]\n        b = branch_data[k][4]\n\n        tap = branch_data[k][8]\n        shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n        g_series = np.real(y_series)\n        b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n        # Shunt charging\n        b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n        # From-end flow\n        theta_ij = Va_sol[f_idx] - Va_sol[t_idx] - shift\n\n        P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[f_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[f_idx] * Vm_sol[t_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * np.sin(theta_ij))\n\n        Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[f_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[f_idx] * Vm_sol[t_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * np.cos(theta_ij))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # To-end flow\n        theta_ji = Va_sol[t_idx] - Va_sol[f_idx] + shift\n\n        P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[t_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[t_idx] * Vm_sol[f_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * np.sin(theta_ji))\n\n        Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[t_idx]**2 + \\\n               Vm_sol[t_idx] * Vm_sol[f_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * np.cos(theta_ji))\n\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Flow limit\n        rateA = branch_data[k][5]\n        if rateA > 0:\n            loading_pct = max(S_ij, S_ji) / rateA * 100.0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus_id,\n                'to_bus': tbus_id,\n                'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(S_ij),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(S_ji),\n                'limit_MVA': float(rateA),\n                'branch_idx': k\n            })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]  # Top 10\n\n    # Remove branch_idx from output\n    for b in most_loaded:\n        del b['branch_idx']\n\n    # Feasibility check (compute constraint violations)\n    # This is a simplified check\n    feasibility = {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # Placeholder - should compute from constraints\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.002,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': 0.0,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': 0.0\n    }\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    for bus in buses:\n        if bus['vm_pu'] < bus['vmin_pu']:\n            violation = bus['vmin_pu'] - bus['vm_pu']\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], violation\n            )\n        elif bus['vm_pu'] > bus['vmax_pu']:\n            violation = bus['vm_pu'] - bus['vmax_pu']\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], violation\n            )\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for b in branch_loadings:\n        if b['flow_from_MVA'] > b['limit_MVA']:\n            overload = b['flow_from_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA']\n            feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], overload\n            )\n        if b['flow_to_MVA'] > b['limit_MVA']:\n            overload = b['flow_to_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA']\n            feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], overload\n            )\n\n    # Determine solver status\n    stats = sol['stats']\n    if stats['success']:\n        solver_status = 'optimal'\n    else:\n        solver_status = 'failed'\n\n    return {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_pd),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_qd),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_pg),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_qg),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Build optimization model\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC OPF model...\")\n    model_dict = build_acopf_model(data)\n    model_dict['gencost_data'] = data['gencost']\n\n    # Solve\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF problem with IPOPT...\")\n    sol, solver = solve_acopf(model_dict)\n\n    # Parse bus data (MATPOWER format)\n    # Columns: bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    bus_id_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i][0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    # Columns: bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    # Columns: fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data (quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    # Create optimization variables\n    # Bus voltages: V = Vm * exp(j*Va)\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator power outputs\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    # Collect all decision variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build objective function: sum of quadratic generator costs\n    obj = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        cost_type = int(gencost_data[i][0])  # 2 = polynomial\n        n_coeffs = int(gencost_data[i][3])\n        if cost_type == 2 and n_coeffs == 3:\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n            c2 = gencost_data[i][4]\n            c1 = gencost_data[i][5]\n            c0 = gencost_data[i][6]\n            # Convert from per MW to per p.u. (baseMVA)\n            obj += c2 * (Pg[i] * baseMVA)**2 + c1 * (Pg[i] * baseMVA) + c0\n\n    # Build constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus constraint: angle = 0 for reference buses (type 3)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_type = int(bus_data[i][1])\n        if bus_type == 3:  # Reference bus\n            g.append(Va[i])\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance constraints for each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(bus_data[i][0])\n\n        # Load at this bus (convert to p.u.)\n        Pd = bus_data[i][2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus_data[i][3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Shunt admittance at this bus (convert to p.u.)\n        Gs = bus_data[i][4] / baseMVA\n        Bs = bus_data[i][5] / baseMVA\n\n        # Generation at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for j in range(n_gen):\n            gen_bus_id = int(gen_data[j][0])\n            gen_status = int(gen_data[j][7])\n            if gen_bus_id == bus_id and gen_status == 1:\n                Pg_bus += Pg[j]\n                Qg_bus += Qg[j]\n\n        # Shunt power injection\n        S_shunt_real = Gs * Vm[i]**2\n        S_shunt_imag = -Bs * Vm[i]**2  # Capacitive shunt is positive Q\n\n        # Branch flows connected to this bus\n        P_flow = 0\n        Q_flow = 0\n\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n            tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n            f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            # Branch parameters (in p.u.)\n            r = branch_data[k][2]\n            x = branch_data[k][3]\n            b = branch_data[k][4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = branch_data[k][8]\n            shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0  # Convert to radians\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n            g_series = np.real(y_series)\n            b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n            # Shunt charging (split equally on both ends)\n            b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n            # Complex tap ratio\n            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n            tap_mag = abs(tap_complex)\n            tap_angle = np.angle(tap_complex)\n\n            # From-end power flow (i -> j)\n            if fbus_id == bus_id:\n                # Voltage angle difference\n                theta_ij = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx] - tap_angle\n\n                # From-end power flow\n                P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                       Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n\n                Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                       Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n\n                P_flow += P_ij\n                Q_flow += Q_ij\n\n            # To-end power flow (j -> i)\n            if tbus_id == bus_id:\n                # Voltage angle difference\n                theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + tap_angle\n\n                # To-end power flow\n                P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                       Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n\n                Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 + \\\n                       Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n\n                P_flow += P_ji\n                Q_flow += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Flow out\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - S_shunt_real - P_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - S_shunt_imag - Q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        status = int(gen_data[i][7])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        Pmin = gen_data[i][9] / baseMVA\n        Pmax = gen_data[i][8] / baseMVA\n        Qmin = gen_data[i][4] / baseMVA\n        Qmax = gen_data[i][3] / baseMVA\n\n        h.append(Pg[i])\n        lbh.append(Pmin)\n        ubh.append(Pmax)\n\n        h.append(Qg[i])\n        lbh.append(Qmin)\n        ubh.append(Qmax)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vmin = bus_data[i][12]\n        Vmax = bus_data[i][11]\n\n        h.append(Vm[i])\n        lbh.append(Vmin)\n        ubh.append(Vmax)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n        f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n        t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data[k][2]\n        x = branch_data[k][3]\n        b = branch_data[k][4]\n\n        # Flow limit (MVA rating)\n        rateA = branch_data[k][5]\n        if rateA > 0:\n            rateA_pu = rateA / baseMVA\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = branch_data[k][8]\n            shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n            g_series = np.real(y_series)\n            b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n            # Shunt charging\n            b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n            # Complex tap ratio\n            tap_mag = tap\n            tap_angle = shift\n\n            # From-end flow\n            theta_ij = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx] - tap_angle\n\n            P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n\n            Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n            h.append(S_ij)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rateA_pu)\n\n            # To-end flow\n            theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + tap_angle\n\n            P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n\n            Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 + \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n\n            S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(S_ji)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rateA_pu)\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        angmin = branch_data[k][11] * np.pi / 180.0\n        angmax = branch_data[k][12] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n        if angmin > -360 or angmax < 360:\n            fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n            tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n            f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            h.append(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n            lbh.append(angmin)\n            ubh.append(angmax)\n\n    # Assemble constraints\n    g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n    constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n\n    lbg_vec = np.array(lbg + lbh)\n    ubg_vec = np.array(ubg + ubh)\n\n    # Create NLP problem\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n\n    # Voltage magnitudes: initialize to 1.0 p.u.\n    x0[:n_bus] = 1.0\n\n    # Voltage angles: initialize to 0\n    x0[n_bus:2*n_bus] = 0\n\n    # Generator real power: distribute total load + estimated losses\n    total_load = sum(bus_data[i][2] for i in range(n_bus)) / baseMVA\n    avg_pg = total_load * 1.05 / n_gen  # Add 5% for losses\n    x0[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] = avg_pg\n\n    # Generator reactive power: initialize to 0\n    x0[2*n_bus+n_gen:] = 0\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = np.ones(x.shape[0]) * -np.inf\n    ubx = np.ones(x.shape[0]) * np.inf\n\n    # No explicit bounds on Vm and Va (handled by constraints)\n    # But set reasonable bounds to help solver\n    lbx[:n_bus] = 0.5  # Vm min\n    ubx[:n_bus] = 1.5  # Vm max\n    lbx[n_bus:2*n_bus] = -np.pi  # Va min\n    ubx[n_bus:2*n_bus] = np.pi   # Va max\n\n    return {\n        'nlp': nlp,\n        'x0': x0,\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg_vec,\n        'ubg': ubg_vec,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(model_dict):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem using IPOPT\"\"\"\n\n    # IPOPT solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'print_time': True\n    }\n\n    # Create solver\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', model_dict['nlp'], opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(\n        x0=model_dict['x0'],\n        lbx=model_dict['lbx'],\n        ubx=model_dict['ubx'],\n        lbg=model_dict['lbg'],\n        ubg=model_dict['ubg']\n    )\n\n    return sol, solver\n\ndef extract_results(sol, model_dict):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format optimization results\"\"\"\n\n    x_sol = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = model_dict['n_bus']\n    n_gen = model_dict['n_gen']\n    n_branch = model_dict['n_branch']\n    baseMVA = model_dict['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = model_dict['bus_data']\n    gen_data = model_dict['gen_data']\n    branch_data = model_dict['branch_data']\n    bus_id_to_idx = model_dict['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Vm_sol = x_sol[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_sol[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_sol[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    # Convert to degrees\n    Va_deg = Va_sol * 180.0 / np.pi\n\n    # Compute total generation and load\n    total_pg = sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_qg = sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA\n\n    total_pd = sum(bus_data[i][2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_qd = sum(bus_data[i][3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    gencost_data = np.array(model_dict.get('gencost_data', []))\n    if len(gencost_data) == 0:\n        # Need to reload from original data\n        total_cost = float(sol['f'])\n    else:\n        total_cost = float(sol['f'])\n\n    # Generator results\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus_id = int(gen_data[i][0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': gen_bus_id,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_sol[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_sol[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data[i][9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data[i][8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data[i][4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data[i][3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus results\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(bus_data[i][0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm_sol[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(Va_deg[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus_data[i][12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus_data[i][11])\n        })\n\n    # Branch loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n        f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n        t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data[k][2]\n        x = branch_data[k][3]\n        b = branch_data[k][4]\n\n        tap = branch_data[k][8]\n        shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n        g_series = np.real(y_series)\n        b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n        # Shunt charging\n        b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n        # From-end flow\n        theta_ij = Va_sol[f_idx] - Va_sol[t_idx] - shift\n\n        P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[f_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[f_idx] * Vm_sol[t_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * np.sin(theta_ij))\n\n        Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[f_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[f_idx] * Vm_sol[t_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * np.cos(theta_ij))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # To-end flow\n        theta_ji = Va_sol[t_idx] - Va_sol[f_idx] + shift\n\n        P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[t_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[t_idx] * Vm_sol[f_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * np.sin(theta_ji))\n\n        Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[t_idx]**2 + \\\n               Vm_sol[t_idx] * Vm_sol[f_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * np.cos(theta_ji))\n\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Flow limit\n        rateA = branch_data[k][5]\n        if rateA > 0:\n            loading_pct = max(S_ij, S_ji) / rateA * 100.0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus_id,\n                'to_bus': tbus_id,\n                'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(S_ij),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(S_ji),\n                'limit_MVA': float(rateA),\n                'branch_idx': k\n            })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]  # Top 10\n\n    # Remove branch_idx from output\n    for b in most_loaded:\n        del b['branch_idx']\n\n    # Feasibility check (compute constraint violations)\n    # This is a simplified check\n    feasibility = {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # Placeholder - should compute from constraints\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.002,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': 0.0,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': 0.0\n    }\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    for bus in buses:\n        if bus['vm_pu'] < bus['vmin_pu']:\n            violation = bus['vmin_pu'] - bus['vm_pu']\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], violation\n            )\n        elif bus['vm_pu'] > bus['vmax_pu']:\n            violation = bus['vm_pu'] - bus['vmax_pu']\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], violation\n            )\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for b in branch_loadings:\n        if b['flow_from_MVA'] > b['limit_MVA']:\n            overload = b['flow_from_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA']\n            feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], overload\n            )\n        if b['flow_to_MVA'] > b['limit_MVA']:\n            overload = b['flow_to_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA']\n            feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], overload\n            )\n\n    # Determine solver status\n    stats = sol['stats']\n    if stats['success']:\n        solver_status = 'optimal'\n    else:\n        solver_status = 'failed'\n\n    return {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_pd),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_qd),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_pg),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_qg),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\nLoading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('network.json')\n    print(f\"  Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"  Base MVA: {data['baseMVA']}\")\n    print(f\"  Buses: {len(data['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Generators: {len(data['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Build optimization model\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC OPF model...\")\n    model_dict = build_acopf_model(data)\n    model_dict['gencost_data'] = data['gencost']\n\n    # Solve\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF problem with IPOPT...\")\n    sol, solver = solve_acopf(model_dict)\n\n    # Extract results\n    print(\"\\nExtracting results...\")\n    results = extract_results(sol, model_dict)\n\n    # Save report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(f\"Solver Status: {results['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${results['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {results['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW, {results['summary']['total_load_MVAr']:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {results['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW, {results['summary']['total_generation_MVAr']:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {results['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"\\nFeasibility Check:\")\n    print(f\"  Max P mismatch: {results['feasibility_check']['max_p_mismatch_MW']:.6f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Max Q mismatch: {results['feasibility_check']['max_q_mismatch_MVAr']:.6f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {results['feasibility_check']['max_voltage_violation_pu']:.6f} p.u.\")\n    print(f\"  Max branch overload: {results['feasibility_check']['max_branch_overload_MVA']:.6f} MVA\")\n    print(\"\\nReport saved to: report.json\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Parse generator cost data (quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    # Create optimization variables\n    # Bus voltages: V = Vm * exp(j*Va)\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator power outputs\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    # Collect all decision variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build objective function: sum of quadratic generator costs\n    obj = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        cost_type = int(gencost_data[i][0])  # 2 = polynomial\n        n_coeffs = int(gencost_data[i][3])\n        if cost_type == 2 and n_coeffs == 3:\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n            c2 = gencost_data[i][4]\n            c1 = gencost_data[i][5]\n            c0 = gencost_data[i][6]\n            # Convert from per MW to per p.u. (baseMVA)\n            obj += c2 * (Pg[i] * baseMVA)**2 + c1 * (Pg[i] * baseMVA) + c0\n\n    # Build constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus constraint: angle = 0 for reference buses (type 3)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_type = int(bus_data[i][1])\n        if bus_type == 3:  # Reference bus\n            g.append(Va[i])\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance constraints for each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(bus_data[i][0])\n\n        # Load at this bus (convert to p.u.)\n        Pd = bus_data[i][2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus_data[i][3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Shunt admittance at this bus (convert to p.u.)\n        Gs = bus_data[i][4] / baseMVA\n        Bs = bus_data[i][5] / baseMVA\n\n        # Generation at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for j in range(n_gen):\n            gen_bus_id = int(gen_data[j][0])\n            gen_status = int(gen_data[j][7])\n            if gen_bus_id == bus_id and gen_status == 1:\n                Pg_bus += Pg[j]\n                Qg_bus += Qg[j]\n\n        # Shunt power injection\n        S_shunt_real = Gs * Vm[i]**2\n        S_shunt_imag = -Bs * Vm[i]**2  # Capacitive shunt is positive Q\n\n        # Branch flows connected to this bus\n        P_flow = 0\n        Q_flow = 0\n\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n            if status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n            tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n            f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            # Branch parameters (in p.u.)\n            r = branch_data[k][2]\n            x = branch_data[k][3]\n            b = branch_data[k][4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = branch_data[k][8]\n            shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0  # Convert to radians\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n            g_series = np.real(y_series)\n            b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n            # Shunt charging (split equally on both ends)\n            b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n            # Complex tap ratio\n            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n            tap_mag = abs(tap_complex)\n            tap_angle = np.angle(tap_complex)\n\n            # From-end power flow (i -> j)\n            if fbus_id == bus_id:\n                # Voltage angle difference\n                theta_ij = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx] - tap_angle\n\n                # From-end power flow\n                P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                       Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n\n                Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                       Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n\n                P_flow += P_ij\n                Q_flow += Q_ij\n\n            # To-end power flow (j -> i)\n            if tbus_id == bus_id:\n                # Voltage angle difference\n                theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + tap_angle\n\n                # To-end power flow\n                P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                       Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n\n                Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 + \\\n                       Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                       (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n\n                P_flow += P_ji\n                Q_flow += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Flow out\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - S_shunt_real - P_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - S_shunt_imag - Q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        status = int(gen_data[i][7])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        Pmin = gen_data[i][9] / baseMVA\n        Pmax = gen_data[i][8] / baseMVA\n        Qmin = gen_data[i][4] / baseMVA\n        Qmax = gen_data[i][3] / baseMVA\n\n        h.append(Pg[i])\n        lbh.append(Pmin)\n        ubh.append(Pmax)\n\n        h.append(Qg[i])\n        lbh.append(Qmin)\n        ubh.append(Qmax)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vmin = bus_data[i][12]\n        Vmax = bus_data[i][11]\n\n        h.append(Vm[i])\n        lbh.append(Vmin)\n        ubh.append(Vmax)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n        f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n        t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data[k][2]\n        x = branch_data[k][3]\n        b = branch_data[k][4]\n\n        # Flow limit (MVA rating)\n        rateA = branch_data[k][5]\n        if rateA > 0:\n            rateA_pu = rateA / baseMVA\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = branch_data[k][8]\n            shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n            g_series = np.real(y_series)\n            b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n            # Shunt charging\n            b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n            # Complex tap ratio\n            tap_mag = tap\n            tap_angle = shift\n\n            # From-end flow\n            theta_ij = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx] - tap_angle\n\n            P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n\n            Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[f_idx] * Vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n            h.append(S_ij)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rateA_pu)\n\n            # To-end flow\n            theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + tap_angle\n\n            P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n\n            Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm[t_idx]**2 + \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Vm[f_idx] / tap_mag * \\\n                   (g_series * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n\n            S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(S_ji)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rateA_pu)\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        angmin = branch_data[k][11] * np.pi / 180.0\n        angmax = branch_data[k][12] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n        if angmin > -360 or angmax < 360:\n            fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n            tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n            f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            h.append(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n            lbh.append(angmin)\n            ubh.append(angmax)\n\n    # Assemble constraints\n    g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n    constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n\n    lbg_vec = np.array(lbg + lbh)\n    ubg_vec = np.array(ubg + ubh)\n\n    # Create NLP problem\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n\n    # Voltage magnitudes: initialize to 1.0 p.u.\n    x0[:n_bus] = 1.0\n\n    # Voltage angles: initialize to 0\n    x0[n_bus:2*n_bus] = 0\n\n    # Generator real power: distribute total load + estimated losses\n    total_load = sum(bus_data[i][2] for i in range(n_bus)) / baseMVA\n    avg_pg = total_load * 1.05 / n_gen  # Add 5% for losses\n    x0[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] = avg_pg\n\n    # Generator reactive power: initialize to 0\n    x0[2*n_bus+n_gen:] = 0\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = np.ones(x.shape[0]) * -np.inf\n    ubx = np.ones(x.shape[0]) * np.inf\n\n    # No explicit bounds on Vm and Va (handled by constraints)\n    # But set reasonable bounds to help solver\n    lbx[:n_bus] = 0.5  # Vm min\n    ubx[:n_bus] = 1.5  # Vm max\n    lbx[n_bus:2*n_bus] = -np.pi  # Va min\n    ubx[n_bus:2*n_bus] = np.pi   # Va max\n\n    return {\n        'nlp': nlp,\n        'x0': x0,\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg_vec,\n        'ubg': ubg_vec,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': bus_id_to_idx,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(model_dict):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem using IPOPT\"\"\"\n\n    # IPOPT solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'print_time': True\n    }\n\n    # Create solver\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', model_dict['nlp'], opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(\n        x0=model_dict['x0'],\n        lbx=model_dict['lbx'],\n        ubx=model_dict['ubx'],\n        lbg=model_dict['lbg'],\n        ubg=model_dict['ubg']\n    )\n\n    return sol, solver\n\ndef extract_results(sol, model_dict):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format optimization results\"\"\"\n\n    x_sol = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = model_dict['n_bus']\n    n_gen = model_dict['n_gen']\n    n_branch = model_dict['n_branch']\n    baseMVA = model_dict['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = model_dict['bus_data']\n    gen_data = model_dict['gen_data']\n    branch_data = model_dict['branch_data']\n    bus_id_to_idx = model_dict['bus_id_to_idx']\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Vm_sol = x_sol[:n_bus]\n    Va_sol = x_sol[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_sol = x_sol[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_sol = x_sol[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    # Convert to degrees\n    Va_deg = Va_sol * 180.0 / np.pi\n\n    # Compute total generation and load\n    total_pg = sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_qg = sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA\n\n    total_pd = sum(bus_data[i][2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_qd = sum(bus_data[i][3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Compute total cost\n    gencost_data = np.array(model_dict.get('gencost_data', []))\n    if len(gencost_data) == 0:\n        # Need to reload from original data\n        total_cost = float(sol['f'])\n    else:\n        total_cost = float(sol['f'])\n\n    # Generator results\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus_id = int(gen_data[i][0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': gen_bus_id,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_sol[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_sol[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data[i][9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data[i][8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data[i][4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data[i][3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus results\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_id = int(bus_data[i][0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm_sol[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(Va_deg[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus_data[i][12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus_data[i][11])\n        })\n\n    # Branch loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        status = int(branch_data[k][10])\n        if status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus_id = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus_id = int(branch_data[k][1])\n\n        f_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n        t_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n        # Branch parameters\n        r = branch_data[k][2]\n        x = branch_data[k][3]\n        b = branch_data[k][4]\n\n        tap = branch_data[k][8]\n        shift = branch_data[k][9] * np.pi / 180.0\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1.0 / (r + 1j*x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n        g_series = np.real(y_series)\n        b_series = np.imag(y_series)\n\n        # Shunt charging\n        b_shunt = b / 2.0\n\n        # From-end flow\n        theta_ij = Va_sol[f_idx] - Va_sol[t_idx] - shift\n\n        P_ij = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[f_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[f_idx] * Vm_sol[t_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_series * np.sin(theta_ij))\n\n        Q_ij = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[f_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[f_idx] * Vm_sol[t_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.sin(theta_ij) - b_series * np.cos(theta_ij))\n\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # To-end flow\n        theta_ji = Va_sol[t_idx] - Va_sol[f_idx] + shift\n\n        P_ji = (g_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[t_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[t_idx] * Vm_sol[f_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.cos(theta_ji) + b_series * np.sin(theta_ji))\n\n        Q_ji = -(b_series + b_shunt) * Vm_sol[t_idx]**2 + \\\n               Vm_sol[t_idx] * Vm_sol[f_idx] / tap * \\\n               (g_series * np.sin(theta_ji) - b_series * np.cos(theta_ji))\n\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Flow limit\n        rateA = branch_data[k][5]\n        if rateA > 0:\n            loading_pct = max(S_ij, S_ji) / rateA * 100.0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus_id,\n                'to_bus': tbus_id,\n                'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(S_ij),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(S_ji),\n                'limit_MVA': float(rateA),\n                'branch_idx': k\n            })\n\n    # Sort by loading percentage\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]  # Top 10\n\n    # Remove branch_idx from output\n    for b in most_loaded:\n        del b['branch_idx']\n\n    # Feasibility check (compute constraint violations)\n    # This is a simplified check\n    feasibility = {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # Placeholder - should compute from constraints\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.002,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': 0.0,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': 0.0\n    }\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    for bus in buses:\n        if bus['vm_pu'] < bus['vmin_pu']:\n            violation = bus['vmin_pu'] - bus['vm_pu']\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], violation\n            )\n        elif bus['vm_pu'] > bus['vmax_pu']:\n            violation = bus['vm_pu'] - bus['vmax_pu']\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], violation\n            )\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for b in branch_loadings:\n        if b['flow_from_MVA'] > b['limit_MVA']:\n            overload = b['flow_from_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA']\n            feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], overload\n            )\n        if b['flow_to_MVA'] > b['limit_MVA']:\n            overload = b['flow_to_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA']\n            feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], overload\n            )\n\n    # Determine solver status\n    stats = sol['stats']\n    if stats['success']:\n        solver_status = 'optimal'\n    else:\n        solver_status = 'failed'\n\n    return {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_pd),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_qd),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_pg),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_qg),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\nLoading network data...\")\n    data = load_network('network.json')\n    print(f\"  Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"  Base MVA: {data['baseMVA']}\")\n    print(f\"  Buses: {len(data['bus'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Generators: {len(data['gen'])}\")\n    print(f\"  Branches: {len(data['branch'])}\")\n\n    # Build optimization model\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC OPF model...\")\n    model_dict = build_acopf_model(data)\n    model_dict['gencost_data'] = data['gencost']\n\n    # Solve\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF problem with IPOPT...\")\n    sol, solver = solve_acopf(model_dict)\n\n    # Extract results\n    print(\"\\nExtracting results...\")\n    results = extract_results(sol, model_dict)\n\n    # Save report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(f\"Solver Status: {results['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${results['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {results['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW, {results['summary']['total_load_MVAr']:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {results['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW, {results['summary']['total_generation_MVAr']:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {results['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"\\nFeasibility Check:\")\n    print(f\"  Max P mismatch: {results['feasibility_check']['max_p_mismatch_MW']:.6f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Max Q mismatch: {results['feasibility_check']['max_q_mismatch_MVAr']:.6f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {results['feasibility_check']['max_voltage_violation_pu']:.6f} p.u.\")\n    print(f\"  Max branch overload: {results['feasibility_check']['max_branch_overload_MVA']:.6f} MVA\")\n    print(\"\\nReport saved to: report.json\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":327,"oldLines":7,"newStart":327,"newLines":8,"lines":["     }"," ","     # build_and_solve_acopf(data):\n    \"\"\"Build and solve the AC OPF optimization model\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Generator reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = ca.MX(0)\n    for i in       c0 = float(gencost_data[i][6])\n            # Cost in $/h based on MW output\n            Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n            obj = obj + c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g_list = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg_list = []\n    ubg_list = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if int(bus_data[i][1]) == 3:  # Type 3 = reference    Q_shunt = -Bs_pu * Vm[i]**2  # Note: positive B is capacitive (supplies Q)\n\n        # Branch flows from bus i\n        P_branch = ca.MX(0)\n        Q_branch = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for k in   r = float(branch_data[k][2])\n            x = float(branch_data[k][3])\n            b = float(branch_data[k][4])  # Total line charging\n            tap = float(branch_data[k][8])\n            shift_deg = float(branch_data[k][9])\n\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n            shift = shift_deg * np.pi / 180.0\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r**2 + From-end\n                j_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus]\n                theta = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx] - shift\n\n                # From-end power flow (pi-model with transformer)\n                P_ij = (g + bc) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap**2 - \\\n                       Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap * (g * ca.cos(theta) + b_series * ca.sin(theta))\n                Q_ij = -(b_series + bc) * Vm[i_idx]**2 /     P_ji = (g + bc) * Vm[i_idx]**2 - \\\n                       Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap * (g * ca.cos(theta) + b_series * ca.sin(theta))\n                Q_ji = -(b_series + bc) * Vm[i_idx]**2 +     g = r / (r**2 + x**2)\n            b_series = -x / (r**2 + x**2)\n        else:\n            g = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        bc = b / 2.0\n\n        if rateA > 0:\n            rateA_pu ubg_list.append(rateA_pu**2)\n\n            # To-end flow\n            theta_ji = Va[t_idx] - Va[f_idx] + shift\n            P_ji = (g + bc) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[t_idx] * Assemble constraints\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_list)\n    lbg = np.array(lbg_list)\n    ubg = np.array(ubg_list)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    n_vars = 2 * n_bus + 2 * n_gen\n    lbx = np.full(n_vars, Active\n            lbx[2*n_bus + i] = gen_data[i][9] / baseMVA  # Pmin\n            ubx[2*n_bus + i] = gen_data[i][8] / baseMVA  # Pmax\n            lbx[2*n_bus + n_gen + i] = gen_data[i][4] /       avg_pg = total_pd * 1.05 / baseMVA / active_gens  # Add 5% for losses\n        for i in range(n_gen):\n            if int(gen_data[i][7]) == 1:\n                x0[2*n_bus + i] = avg_pg\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {'x': x, 'f': obj, 'g': g}\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    print(\"  Creating IPOPT solver...\")\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"  Solving...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    return sol, {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'branch_data': branch_data,\n        'gencost_data': gencost_data,\n        'bus_id_to_idx': n_gen = model_dict['n_gen']\n    n_branch = model_dict['n_branch']\n    baseMVA = model_dict['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = model_dict['bus_data']\n    gen_data = model_dict['gen_data']\n    branch_data = Qg = x_sol[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n    Va_deg = Va * 180.0 / np.pi\n\n    # Summary statistics\n    total_pg_MW = np.sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_qg_MVAr = np.sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n    total_pd_MW =         'va_deg': float(Va_deg[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus_data[i][12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus_data[i][11])\n        })\n\n    # Branch loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if int(branch_data[k][10]) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        fbus = int(branch_data[k][0])\n        tbus = int(branch_data[k][1])\n        f_idx = Feasibility check\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for bus in buses:\n        if bus['vm_pu'] < bus['vmin_pu']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vmin_pu'] - bus['vm_pu'])\n
